# Skills · E · part 36

840 entries, elo to email. [Index](README.md) · [Everything](../README.md)

- **elo**: ELO — chat principal (o CEO dos chats do ERP Line). Use quando o pedido for sobre organizar os chats, dar a sigla de 3 letras a um chat novo, escrever ou atualizar a skill de um chat, a lista de chats em Sobre → Chats (tabela chats_claude), o mapa de chats do CLAUDE.md ou para qual chat levar um assunto. Diz o que este chat faz, o que leva para outro chat e as regras que não pode esquecer. ([mfmaquinasdesorvete-lang/mf-m-erp](https://github.com/mfmaquinasdesorvete-lang/mf-m-erp/tree/HEAD/.claude/skills/elo) · ★1)

- **elo-assets**: Use when an AI agent (Claude Code, Codex, Cursor, Hermes, OpenClaw, any harness with a shell) should find and add game assets from the Elo Pros asset store to a project — models, sprites, sounds, shaders, plugins, each 99¢ or less, with the licence and credit line written for you — or make an asset (for example a model in Blender through a Blender MCP server), put it in a GitHub release, write its elo-asset.json and list it. No wallet is needed to list; Elo never holds a key. ([AnthonE/elo-pros-launcher](https://github.com/AnthonE/elo-pros-launcher/tree/HEAD/skills/elo-assets) · ★1)
- **elo-builder**: Use when an AI agent (Hermes, Claude Code, Codex, OpenClaw, any harness with a shell and git) should take paid work on Elo Pros — read the board, open a pull request to the Gates repo, and deliver it to get paid the board's posted price; paint a texture skin for a Gates item with skinkit and ship it as pull requests; or act as a listed game's own agent on the dev desk. - **elo-by-weight-class**: Reads and uses FighterAPI's Elo fighter ratings by weight class: leaderboards of active fighters, rating gaps as win probabilities, peak vs current, rating change per fight, and strength of schedule. Use when the user asks who is rated highest at a weight, how big a rating gap is, who is underrated or overrated, or how a fighter's rating moved. Do not use for official rankings -- those are not in the data. Do not use for market prices -- see odds-explorer. Do not use to check calibration -- see probability-calibration.
